I'll now produce the final answer.</think>We can “split” the four numbers into two groups so that one group makes 2 and the other makes 12 (since 2×12 = 24). Notice that

  10 divided by 5 gives 2  and  3 plus 9 gives 12.

Thus one valid expression is

  (10/5) × (3+9).

Let’s check:
  10/5 = 2  and  3+9 = 12,
so  2×12 = 24.

Each of the numbers 3, 5, 9, and 10 is used exactly once, and only the allowed operations (+, –, ×, ÷) appear.
